## 2. Preserved Glossary & Sacred Terminology Policy

All of the following terms are deliberately preserved according to authentic yoga tradition and international accreditation standards:

| Term | Category | Policy / Handling | Rationale |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **YogaGarhi** | Brand | Preserved verbatim | Official registered global brand name |
| **Sachin Ji / Sachin** | Teacher Name | Preserved verbatim (or transliterated) | Lead Master Teacher name with respectful honorific "Ji" |
| **Yash** | Teacher Name | Preserved verbatim (or transliterated) | Founder & Philosophy Teacher |
| **Asana** | Sanskrit Term | Preserved in Roman script or standard transliteration | Universal physical posture terminology in Yoga |
| **Pranayama** | Sanskrit Term | Preserved in Roman script or standard transliteration | Breath control / life force regulation |
| **Hatha / Vinyasa / Ashtanga** | Yoga Traditions | Preserved in Roman script or standard transliteration | Core yogic lineage styles recognized worldwide |
| **Sadhana** | Sanskrit Term | Preserved with context explanation | Dedicated spiritual discipline / inner practice |
| **Agni Hotra** | Sacred Ritual | Preserved verbatim | Ancient Vedic sacred fire purification ceremony |
| **Sattvic** | Ayurvedic Concept | Preserved or paired with pure/nourishing | Ayurvedic dietary principle of purity and harmony |
| **Kirtan / Bhakti** | Sanskrit Concept | Preserved verbatim | Devotional chanting and devotional path of yoga |
| **Dosha / Prakriti** | Ayurvedic Science | Preserved verbatim | Bio-energetic constitutions and individual constitutional nature |
| **Mudra / Mantra** | Yogic Practice | Preserved verbatim | Energetic gestures and sacred sound resonance |
| **Yoga Alliance / RYT 200 / RYS 200** | Certification | Preserved verbatim | International professional accreditation standard |
| **$999 / $1,750 / $1,899 / $2,499** | Pricing | Preserved USD symbol & numbers | Official tuition and accommodation tiers |

## 3. Top 5 Culturally Sensitive / Nuanced Strings Flagged for Native Review

These 5 strings carry deep philosophical, spiritual, or commercial weight and have been translated with high care. Reviewers should confirm the tone aligns with local spiritual and yoga communities:

| # | Focus Area | English Source | Target Translation | Back-Translation | Linguistic Review Rationale |
| :-: | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **1** | **1-Year Post-Graduation Mentorship Promise** (`home.whyChoose.postTtcDesc`) | Most schools say goodbye on graduation day. We don't. For 12 months after your training, you get monthly live sessions with us — feedback on your real classes if you teach, guidance on your personal practice if you don't — and a teacher you can write to when you're stuck. This isn't a new idea we invented for marketing: students from our founder's past trainings have been practicing with him online for three, four, even five years after their course ended. We simply made into a promise what was already true. | 대부분의 학교는 졸업식 날로 모든 관계가 끝납니다. 하지만 저희는 다릅니다. 수료 후 12개월 동안 매달 라이브 온라인 세션을 통해 여러분과 만납니다. 티칭을 시작하신 분께는 실제 수업 시퀀스에 대한 피드백을, 개인 수련에 집중하시는 분께는 sadhana 가이드를 제공합니다. 이는 단순한 마케팅이 아닙니다. 설립 강사의 수강생들은 수료 후 3년, 4년, 5년이 지난 지금도 온라인에서 함께 수련하고 있습니다. 저희는 원래 하던 따뜻한 인연을 약속으로 문서화했을 뿐입니다. | Most schools say goodbye on graduation day. We don't. For 12 months after your training, you get monthly live sessions with us — feedback on your real classes if you teach, guidance on your personal practice if you don't — and a teacher you can write to when you're stuck. This isn't a new idea we invented for marketing: students from our founder's past trainings have been practicing with him online for three, four, even five years after their course ended. We simply made into a promise what was already true. | Expresses the school's core 12-month mentorship commitment with monthly live video guidance and personal feedback. |
| **2** | **Student Manifesto / Pure Practice Nuance** (`home.whyChoose.manifestoQuote`) | "I don't actually want to teach. I just want to go deeper." | "저는 강사가 되려는 게 아니라, 그저 제 요가를 깊이 알고 싶을 뿐이에요." | "I don't actually want to teach. I just want to go deeper." | High philosophical nuance distinguishing commercial fitness certification from traditional authentic sadhana transmission. |
| **3** | **Philosophy of Self-Discovery (Non-Career Focus)** (`home.whyChoose.manifestoIntro`) | More than half the people who write to us say this, almost apologetically. Please don't apologize. In India, yoga was never a career course — it was a pathway to understand oneself. The student always came first. | 저희에게 문의하시는 분의 절반 이상이 조심스럽게 이렇게 말씀하십니다. 전혀 미안해하실 필요가 없습니다. 원래 인도에서 요가는 취업 준비 과정이 아니라, 진정한 나를 찾아가는 구도의 길이었습니다. 시대가 변해도 가장 중요한 것은 언제나 수련자 자신의 내적 성장입니다. | More than half the people who write to us say this, almost apologetically. Please don't apologize. In India, yoga was never a career course — it was a pathway to understand oneself. The student always came first. | Reframing yoga teacher training as personal self-realization rather than a commercial career course. |
| **4** | **Sacred Vedic Fire Ceremony (Agni Hotra)** (`course200.inclusions.incCeremonies`) | Traditional opening and closing fire ceremonies (Agni Hotra) | 전통 개강 및 종강 화제의식 (Agni Hotra) | Traditional opening and closing fire ceremonies (Agni Hotra) | Preservation of ancient Vedic sacred fire purification rituals and spiritual tradition. |
| **5** | **Sacred Waterfall Cleansing Ceremony (Melukat)** (`course200.excursionsList[2].description`) | Journey to a sacred waterfall for a traditional cleansing ritual. Let the pure waters wash away what no longer serves you. | 신성한 자연 폭포를 찾아 맑은 샘물로 몸과 마음의 묵은 때를 씻어내는 정화 의식을 경험합니다. | Journey to a sacred waterfall for a traditional cleansing ritual. Let the pure waters wash away what no longer serves you. | Balinese Hindu purification ritual at a sacred holy water temple; requires deep cultural respect and sacred phrasing. |

| Homepage: Why YogaGarhi Pillars | Most schools say goodbye on graduation day. We don't. | 200hr Bali: overview | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | 발리 200시간 요가 지도자 과정은 전문 강사를 꿈꾸는 분뿐만 아니라 자신의 수련과 삶을 깊이 변화시키고자 하는 초급~중급 수련자를 위한 집중 과정입니다. 우붓 인근 기안야르의 평화로운 아쉬람에서 매일 아사나를 수련하고, 바른 정렬, 호흡법, 명상, 요가 철학, 해부학, 티칭 기법을 체계적으로 익힙니다. | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Seamless integration of classical Sanskrit terminology within KO grammatical structure. |

| 100hr Bali YTTC | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | 발리 200시간 요가 지도자 과정은 전문 강사를 꿈꾸는 분뿐만 아니라 자신의 수련과 삶을 깊이 변화시키고자 하는 초급~중급 수련자를 위한 집중 과정입니다. 우붓 인근 기안야르의 평화로운 아쉬람에서 매일 아사나를 수련하고, 바른 정렬, 호흡법, 명상, 요가 철학, 해부학, 티칭 기법을 체계적으로 익힙니다. | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. | 100hr Rishikesh YTTC | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. | 200hr Rishikesh YTTC | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | 발리 200시간 요가 지도자 과정은 전문 강사를 꿈꾸는 분뿐만 아니라 자신의 수련과 삶을 깊이 변화시키고자 하는 초급~중급 수련자를 위한 집중 과정입니다. 우붓 인근 기안야르의 평화로운 아쉬람에서 매일 아사나를 수련하고, 바른 정렬, 호흡법, 명상, 요가 철학, 해부학, 티칭 기법을 체계적으로 익힙니다. | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life.
